- **readme-review**: Review a single canonical README and judge whether it is manual-worthy for the portal manifest curated per ADR 0141. Produces a scorecard and a verdict (manual-worthy / borderline / not-yet-manual-grade / out-of-scope-for-portal). The criteria are derived at runtime from the currently-registered entries, and exclude the two shapes deliberately not portal content: the per-component reference READMEs Storybook and TSDoc already carry, and feature slices graded by their own required-section check.
